# QA_MASTER — HBL-XWeb system QA traceability master (C6)

> **Single source of truth for QA from now to production.** Generated 2026-10-07 00:07 UTC by `docs/parallel/c6/harness/qa_master.py` (+ `qa_master_reqs.py`) for **HEAD `8e91172`** (previous tested `f894cc6`).
> Chain proven here: **Requirement/Contract clause → Test case → Result → Bug → Retest → Evidence.** Two dimensions per requirement: **Implementation** (is it in the product?) and **Verification** (did QA prove it?). It does **not** replace raw evidence and never deletes history: per-row history, bug lifecycle events and GAP ids live in `docs/parallel/c6/qa_master_state.json` (append-only). Linked docs: [QA_STATUS](QA_STATUS.md) · [BUGS](BUGS.md) · [REGRESSION_MATRIX](REGRESSION_MATRIX.md) · [E2E_MATRIX](E2E_MATRIX.md).
> Rules: status of automated rows is **derived** from the per-test-case JUnit export, the C6 check outputs or the overlay results of the SHA under test; never PASS on old-SHA or cached evidence; requirement Coverage/Result/Verification are **computed**; Implementation is explicit and evidenced (code reference, branch + SHA, or tests executing the clause). **No mock is ever counted as verification of an unintegrated feature.**

## 1. Verdict

**QA = YELLOW · Production Ready = NO.** Product bugs: 0 new. Open bugs: 8 (all Low: docs/config/harness). Requirements: 191 — Coverage FULL 115 · PARTIAL 48 · NONE 28; Implementation: IMPLEMENTED 128 · PARTIAL 54 · NOT_IMPLEMENTED 8 · UNKNOWN 1; Verification: VERIFIED 117 · PARTIAL 22 · UNVERIFIED 4 · BLOCKED 48.

**QA blockers** (P0, IMPLEMENTED, no verification): **0**. **P0 blocked by unfinished implementation** (not a QA failure): **30**. P0 blocked by environment: 0. A P0 requirement that is NOT_IMPLEMENTED and untested is Verification BLOCKED and is **not** a QA failure; an IMPLEMENTED P0 requirement without verification **is** a QA blocker.

Legend (test rows): ✅ PASS · ❌ FAIL · ⚠ BLOCKED (cannot run: stack/feature missing) · ○ NOT RUN · 🔁 RETEST REQUIRED (not valid for the current SHA) · 🟦 GAP (no test exists). Gate/requirement result model: **PASS · PASS_WITH_GAPS · FAIL · BLOCKED · NOT_RUN**. Implementation: IMPLEMENTED (on integration/v2) · PARTIAL (part on integration/v2, or complete only on an unintegrated branch) · NOT_IMPLEMENTED (no code anywhere) · UNKNOWN. Verification: VERIFIED · PARTIAL · UNVERIFIED (implemented, no/insufficient test) · BLOCKED (by implementation or environment). P0 = a failure can cause: auth/security isolation breach, privilege escalation, secret/credential leak, data loss/corruption, duplicate unsafe mutation, broken idempotency, ambiguous workflow mutation, unsafe replay, migration corruption, publish/CAS race, rollback to wrong runtime, critical recovery inconsistency. P1 core flows · P2 edge cases · P3 cosmetic.

Evidence keys: **J** = `evidence/mac-8e91172-gate/backend-testcases.tsv` (1502 per-case rows; XML in `backend-junit-xml.tgz`) · **NS** = `evidence/mac-8e91172-nostack/RESULTS.txt` · **SQ** = `evidence/mac-8e91172-nostack/STATIC_QA.log` · **C6** = `evidence/mac-8e91172-contract-checks/` (`contract-checks.tsv` static checks with a self-test, `db-probes.tsv` throw-away PostgreSQL 17.6, `TEST-*.xml` overlay Gradle run of C6-owned HTTP tests, `npm-audit-prod.json`) · **R1** = `evidence/mac-run1-cached-20261006T1323Z/` (f894cc6; backend result INVALID cache replay) · **G2** = `evidence/mac-gate2-nocache-kotlin-oom-20261006T1331Z/`.

## 7. GAP management

Every GAP/BLOCKED test row has a persistent GAP ID (GAP-C6-01…06 are the original ids from `E2E_MATRIX.md`/`BUGS.md`). *Cause*: **QA coverage** (feature exists, no test), **implementation** (feature missing or not integrated), **environment** (needs the stack/ports). *Blocking gate* = the gate that cannot be PASS while it is open. *Required before RC* = YES for P0/P1 and for P2 in gates G3/G5/G6/G7.

## 10. HEAD change policy and impact of the last change

For every new integration/v2 HEAD:
1. Record the exact SHA and diff against the last tested SHA (the generator runs `git diff --name-only <prev> <sha>` and lists impacted requirements below).
2. Identify impacted requirements (path rules in `qa_master.py` `IMPACT`) and impacted tests (`Watch` = YES and the suites of changed modules).
3. Re-run affected tests; **always re-run the C6 checks** (`harness/contract-checks.py`, `harness/db-probes.py`, `harness/run-overlay-tests.sh`) — they are cheap and catch layering, audit, migration-immutability and permission regressions; security regression if contract/auth/data/action changed; full backend uncached only for migration/core/shared wiring changes (`C6_GRADLE_ARGS` defaults to `-Pkotlin.daemon.jvmargs=-Xmx3g`; the gate refuses `FROM-CACHE`).
4. Export per-case results, regenerate: `python3 docs/parallel/c6/harness/qa_master.py --sha <new> --prev <prev> --tsv <tsv> --nostack <dir> --checks <dir> --write-state`.
5. Never reuse old-SHA evidence as a new PASS: rows not re-run show their old *Tested SHA* or 🔁 RETEST REQUIRED.
6. Bug found → add to `BUGS` in `qa_master.py` + `BUGS.md`, route to the owner (C1 IAM/security · C2 build/publish/deploy · C3 data/connectors · C4 action/workflow/queue · C5 Studio/frontend · C0 integration/migration/persistence), link the failing row. After the fix: retest first, then RESOLVED. History is never removed.
7. When a feature is integrated (Management API, V30, RabbitMQ queue, apiBase) its requirements change from BLOCKED(implementation) to testable: update `IMPL`/`BLOCKED_BY_IMPLEMENTATION` in `qa_master_reqs.py` in the same commit that records the integration SHA, then run the real tests — never earlier.
8. Triggers: C3 GREEN → data/management/connector/security/atomicity · C0 integrates C3 → integration + migration/persistence · C2 V30/apiBase → publish/deploy/rollback/runtime · C5 real E2E unblocked → full real E2E · RC → full final QA (backend no-cache, frontend, security, real E2E, browser, recovery, performance).

## 11. P0 priority review (batch 7)

Criteria: a test is P0 only if its failure can cause **auth/security isolation breach, privilege escalation, secret/credential leak, data loss/corruption, duplicate unsafe mutation, broken idempotency, ambiguous workflow/mutation outcome, unsafe replay, migration corruption, publish/CAS race, rollback to wrong runtime/state, critical recovery inconsistency**. The 212 P0 test cases of batch 6 were audited one by one against these criteria, and the rows that batch 6 had demoted by "critical functional" reasoning were re-checked against the SAME criteria. Result: **212 → 233 P0 test cases** (10 demoted to P1; 29 existing rows promoted to P0 because they match the criteria; 2 new P0 rows: C2-DEF-17, C3-SRC-09). Every P0 row carries a risk class (the code in the *Pri* column). The count is not a target: promotions are as evidence-based as demotions.

| Class | Meaning | P0 test cases |
|---|---|---|
| AUTH | auth/security isolation breach | 85 |
| PRIV | privilege escalation | 13 |
| SECRET | secret/credential leak | 13 |
| LOSS | data loss/corruption | 16 |
| DUP | duplicate unsafe mutation | 27 |
| IDEM | broken idempotency | 14 |
| AMBIG | ambiguous workflow/mutation outcome | 16 |
| REPLAY | unsafe replay | 8 |
| MIG | migration corruption | 4 |
| CAS | publish/CAS race | 10 |
| ROLLBACK | rollback to wrong runtime/state | 8 |
| RECOV | critical recovery inconsistency | 19 |

| Demoted P0 → P1 | Reason |
|---|---|
| C0-ARCH-04 legacy project/publish/static-site/server-runtime/audit APIs unchanged | regression of legacy APIs: functional break, no isolation/data-loss path |
| C1-AUD-03 audit failure at STARTED fails closed (action never runs); audit failure after r | an unaudited action is an audit-completeness gap, not a breach; the audit-failure fail-closed rule is functional hardening |
| C2-DEF-01 validator rejects duplicate ids, unknown component version, missing required pro | invalid app definitions are rejected/accepted at the validator: functional correctness, versions stay immutable (C2-DEF-05/10 stay P0) |
| C2-DEF-06 AppDefinition V2 validator: error codes, references, limits | V2 validator error codes: functional correctness |
| C3-MUT-08 production writable connector: real INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE against an external DB | a writable production connector is a missing feature; the safety semantics are P0 in C3-MUT-02/10 |
| C5-STU-03 save with a stale revision shows a conflict and does not overwrite (browser, rea | the server already prevents the lost update (C2-DEF-02 P0); the UI message is functional |
| C5-STU-06 unauthorised user in the browser: forbidden UI states and server denial agree (v | the server is the authority and is P0-tested; browser/server agreement is UX |
| REC-R06 Redis down/up: readiness drops, session store recovers | Redis outage logs users out and recovers; no data loss or inconsistency path |
| C3-MGT-09 data source create 201 / PATCH all-or-nothing (one transaction, one version bump | management CRUD happy path is functional; its dangerous parts are C3-MGT-10 (P0) |
| C3-MGT-18 audit: every change writes an audit row (ids/versions/hash, never body/credentia | audit-with-write unit is audit completeness; credential/tenant leaks are P0 elsewhere |

| Promoted P1 → P0 | Class | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| C0-MIG-01 the three run tables exist and tenant_id is mandatory on all | AUTH | tenant_id NOT NULL on every run table is the database-level isolation key |
| C0-MIG-05 contract columns: action_runs.mutating default TRUE and mandatory; workflow leas | AMBIG | mutating defaults TRUE (A-1): the safe default for an unspecified run decides whether an ambiguous write is ever retried |
| C0-MIG-11 every data-runtime table exists with mandatory tenant_id | AUTH | tenant_id NOT NULL on every data-runtime table |
| C0-MIG-18 legacy INSERT keeps working; mismatching tenant_id rejected on migrated rows | AUTH | a mismatching tenant_id on migrated rows must be rejected (isolation), not only legacy INSERT compatibility |
| C0-WIR-15 status codes follow contract table; error body shape; unknown outcome 409 never  | AMBIG | unknown outcome is 409 and never retryable, rejected mutation 422: the HTTP face of the ambiguity rules |
| C0-PER-06 completed mutation replay survives a new store instance (restart) | IDEM | a completed mutation's idempotency replay survives a new store instance (restart): a retry after a restart writes nothing |
| C0-PER-22 concurrent creates of one key produce exactly one run; create idempotent per sco | IDEM | concurrent creates of one key produce exactly one workflow run |
| C1-AUTH-02 wrong password is 401; repeated failures end in 429 with Retry-After | AUTH | failed-login throttling is the control against credential guessing |
| C1-AUTH-04 logout invalidates the session; session stored in Redis; cookie HttpOnly | AUTH | logout must invalidate the server-side session |
| C1-PRM-13 write types need DATA_MUTATE in addition to ACTION_EXECUTE; permission codes are | PRIV | write action types need DATA_MUTATE on top of ACTION_EXECUTE |
| C1-PRM-15 authorizer / tenant gate / access port outage fails closed (deny or retryable de | AUTH | an authorizer/tenant-gate outage must deny, never allow |
| C1-SEC-08 stale or future timestamps rejected even when the signature is valid | REPLAY | stale or future timestamps are rejected even with a valid signature (replay window) |
| C1-SEC-14 infrastructure failures answer 503 and never accept unchecked; failed delivery r | AUTH | webhook infrastructure failure must answer 503 and never accept an unchecked delivery |
| C1-SEC-21 connector proxy needs gateway token, valid app token, grant and declared operati | AUTH | connector proxy needs gateway token, app token, grant and a declared operation; secrets write-only |
| C2-DEF-09 backward compatibility with V1 Page Schema and STATIC_APP | LOSS | legacy Page Schema documents must read and round-trip unchanged (no silent rewrite of stored documents) |
| C2-PUB-04 concurrent duplicate requests with one key create exactly one deployment | CAS | concurrent duplicate publish requests with one key create exactly one deployment |
| C2-DEP-13 real rollback on the stack serves previous artifact | ROLLBACK | real rollback on the stack must serve the previous artifact |
| C3-QRY-07 answer computed before an invalidation cannot be stored after it (stress, tenant | AUTH | cache tickets are tenant-bound: another tenant's entry can never be filled; no stale answer after invalidation |
| C3-IDM-03 key mandatory and well formed; only derived shape sha256/base64url accepted | IDEM | the idempotency key is mandatory and only the derived shape is accepted |
| C3-IDM-07 store failure after a successful write does not turn success into error; store f | AMBIG | a store failure after a successful write must not become an error; one while recording an ambiguous failure must keep the key unusable |
| C3-WHK-03 endpoint bound only to things its creator can reference; administration needs pe | AUTH | webhook endpoints bind only to things their creator can reference; administration needs its permission |
| C4-ACT-11 chain cycle stopped by depth limit; fan-out bounded; chained writes get derived  | IDEM | chained writes get derived keys so re-running a chain is idempotent |
| C4-ACT-15 derived key is base64url sha256 (43 chars), user/tenant-separated, stable; raw c | IDEM | derived idempotency key (sha256/base64url, user/tenant separated) and raw keys never forwarded |
| C4-ACT-20 run store: concurrent begins yield exactly one owner; stale owner cannot complet | DUP | concurrent begins of one key yield exactly one owner; a stale owner cannot complete |
| C4-WFL-12 approval step pauses run; quorum; rejection fails run; only snapshotted approver | PRIV | only snapshotted approvers decide, any rejection rejects, a late decision cannot revive an expired request |
| C4-LSE-02 only the owner renews; owner that keeps renewing is not reclaimed, one that stop | DUP | only the owner renews a lease; a stale renewal does not extend it (fencing) |
| C4-SCH-01 many workers ticking concurrently hand every fire over exactly once; claiming fr | DUP | concurrent ticks hand every fire over exactly once |
| C4-SCH-04 failing enqueue backs off exponentially and is given up after attempt budget; le | DUP | failed enqueue, unavailable ledger and lost confirmation never produce a second hand-over |
| C4-REC-02 duplicated workflow start (same key, parallel) over HTTP creates one run | IDEM | duplicated workflow start with one key over HTTP creates one run |

| P0 requirement demoted to P1 | Reason |
|---|---|
| REQ-RA-14 | HTTP envelope/status table is functional; the dangerous mappings (unknown outcome, rejected mutation) are REQ-RA-15 (P0) |
| REQ-DR-05 | local->runtime id resolution is functional correctness; isolation is enforced afterwards by the gateway (REQ-DR-16, P0) |
| REQ-AD-12 | local ids/translation rule is structural; isolation is REQ-DR-16 |
| REQ-IC-01 | package layering is architecture hygiene, not an allowed P0 outcome |

Rule enforced by the generator: every P0 requirement keeps at least one P0 test row; every P0 test row has a risk class.

## 12. Corrections log (GAP/BLOCKED rows re-evidenced or re-classified in batch 7)

The batch-6 GAP detection searched test **names** only. Reading test **bodies** and the product code found existing coverage and wrongly-labelled gaps. Each row below changed because of evidence, not to improve a count.

| Test ID | Was | Now | Evidence / reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1-PRM-07 | GAP | ✅ PASS | C6 overlay HTTP test: a project VIEWER (APP_USE only) gets 403 FORBIDDEN naming QUERY_EXECUTE on the LIVE route and on TEST; a workspace admin is not refused |
| C1-PRM-10 | GAP | ✅ PASS | C6 overlay HTTP test: an EDITOR (APP_USE+ACTION_EXECUTE, no WORKFLOW_EXECUTE) gets 403 FORBIDDEN on workflow start, no run row is created; admin control is not refused |
| C1-SEC-22 | GAP | ✅ PASS | C6 ran `npm audit --omit=dev --json` (the repo's scripts/check.sh step): 0 vulnerabilities. The BACKEND has no scan: see C1-SEC-28 |
| C1-AUD-01 | GAP | ✅ PASS | existing tests assert UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE are refused for the application user (AuditApiTests, ProjectApiTests); C6 adds the app-layer scan and a DB probe incl. a non-owner role and a negative control. Residual (owner can disable the trigger) = C1-AUD-06 / T19 |
| C3-MUT-09 | GAP | ⚠ BLOCKED | re-classified GAP -> BLOCKED(implementation): there is no production write path to test |
| C1-TEN-22 | GAP | ✅ PASS | the test asserts 403 TENANT_SUSPENDED (service) and HTTP 403 for a suspended tenant, then 200 when re-activated |
| C1-TEN-23 | GAP | ✅ PASS | C6 static check (self-tested): every AccessContext construction passes tenantId+tenantContext explicitly; DEFAULT tenant only in 4 audited files; tenant derived from the resolver |
| C1-PRM-24 | GAP | ✅ PASS | the test asserts 404 on project detail/schema/versions and an EMPTY project list for a non-member system admin |
| C1-SEC-25 | GAP | ⚠ BLOCKED | re-classified GAP -> BLOCKED(implementation): grep ai/tenant finds no PublicAddress use |
| C0-WIR-18 | GAP | ✅ PASS | C6 static check: no controller maps /api/v1/data/** or any *mutate* route; app-runtime family is exactly the two controllers; flags-OFF test already returns 404/405 |
| C0-ARCH-07 | GAP | ✅ PASS | C6 static layering check of integration-contract section 1 over all main imports (self-tested with injected violations) |
| C0-MIG-25 | GAP | ✅ PASS | C6: V1..V28 byte-identical between f894cc6 and 8e91172, all 29 hashes pinned in docs/parallel/c6/migration-hashes.sha256 |
| C0-MIG-27 | GAP | ✅ PASS | C6 DB probe on a throw-away PostgreSQL 17.6: U27 proceeds on backfill-only rows, refuses edited/approved rows, drops when empty |
| C2-PUB-14 | GAP | ✅ PASS | the test adopts a draft over an existing configuration with the current revision (fromDraft/CAS) and refuses a PUBLIC draft without confirmation |
| C2-PUB-15 | GAP | ⚠ BLOCKED | re-classified GAP -> BLOCKED(implementation) |
| C4-RET-02 | GAP | ⚠ BLOCKED | re-classified GAP -> BLOCKED(implementation) |
